What he saw on the DECK BAR is Slate's own default navigation, not ours: each of the ten `UDeckSlotEntryWidget` entries wraps a `UButton` (`SlotButton`, focusable by default), so arrows move Slate focus along the bar (the dashed focus rectangle — ⛔ not the orange active outline) and Enter on a focused slot fires `SlotButton->OnClicked` = `OnLeftClicked` = `SelectDeckForEdit` (the editing tint) — never the right-click/active meaning. Why focus does not continue into the card grid is UNMEASURED (needs a read-only look at `WBP_DeckCardTile`); hypotheses (`SC-§101`): (a) the tile's three buttons have `Is Focusable` unchecked, (b) focus DOES reach `Btn_CardFace` but it is a transparent style-less button on which the 50 %-alpha dashed rectangle is invisible over card art, (c) the grid's container blocks directional navigation. He did not miss it: as shipped, there is nothing to find. ⇒ feature ask boarded as `TASK-1286` (`backlog`, ⛔ needs his go).

    **Why (one line):** five of seven main-menu flows (deck builder, sandbox, settings, login, multiplayer) are verifier-blind and stay `UNOBSERVABLE` forever (`qa/TASK-671-verify.md`), while `inject_input_action` reached OUR mappings on every leg that used it (`IA_Move`, `IA_Card1`, `IA_DiscardAll`) — a menu mapping context is the cheapest door, and it doubles as keyboard/gamepad menu navigation for players. **Why not (the honest side):** the defect is in Aura's injection path (`ui_perform` `down handled=true / up handled=false`, keys delivered to the player controller, not Slate) ⭐⛔ **[RIDER 2026-09-24, ⭐ `TASK-1460`, marker `CLICK-LANE-CEILING-RULED-2026-09-24`: the parenthetical's SECOND half is now ⛔ MEASURED rather than inferred — ⭐ `TASK-1459` D1 showed `simulate_key_press` delivers into ⛔ ENHANCED INPUT and ⛔ NOT into Slate (⛔ `SpaceBar`, ⛔ unbound in `IMC_MainMenu`, ⛔ did nothing; the ⛔ same button opened on `inject_input_action IA_MenuAccept`). ⛔ Its FIRST half is an ⛔ ARTEFACT: `down handled` is a ⛔ hardcoded `return true;` (`SlateApplication.cpp:5411`) ⇒ read it ⛔ ONLY as *the up failed*, ⛔ never as *the down worked*.]**, so this is engineering AROUND a vendor tool; a real mouse click and a real keyboard Tab are unaffected today. **Deliverables:** (1) assets, existing patterns (`IMC_Hero` / `IA_Card1` — no new asset type): `/Game/Input/IMC_MainMenu` + `/Game/Input/Actions/IA_MenuUp`, `IA_MenuDown`, `IA_MenuAccept` (Boolean); default keys = Up/Down arrows + gamepad D-pad, Accept = Enter + gamepad FaceButtonBottom — ⛔ no letters (so `KBD-§4`'s remap table is never entered), ⛔ Tab untouched (Slate's own). (2) the class that runs on `L_MainMenu` (measure it: N3 read `DefaultPawn_0`, `applied_mapping_contexts: []` — quote the game mode / player controller class) adds `IMC_MainMenu` at BeginPlay on `L_MainMenu` ONLY; handlers: Up/Down move Slate focus (`SetUserFocus`) across `Overlay_19/VerticalBox_0/Button_*` in order, wrapping; Accept calls the focused `UButton`'s `OnClicked.Broadcast()` — the SAME delegate a mouse click fires, so every existing Blueprint handler runs unchanged. ⛔ No button's `OnClicked` body changes; ⛔ `WBP_MainMenu`'s Construct is not rewritten. (3) a visible focus state on the focused button (Slate's default focus brush is acceptable — say which). **Tests:** one automation test asserting STATE (`SC-§104`): after `IA_MenuDown` ×2 from cold, the focused widget's text is "Deck Builder"; after `IA_MenuAccept`, a `UDeckBuilderWidget` exists.

What was actually measured is the verifier's `ui_perform` control line on `TASK-1281` cl.     ⛔ **BLOCK 1 — ⛔ NOTHING EVER PUTS THE BUILDER ON THE FOCUS PATH.** `FSlateApplication::ProcessKeyDownEvent` routes ⛔ only along `SlateUser->GetFocusPath()` (`SlateApplication.cpp:4961`, tunnel `:5024`, bubble `:5047`); the ⛔ one fallback (`:5068`) forwards to the ⛔ player controller — i.e. ⛔ Enhanced Input, ⛔ NOT Slate. ⇒ `NativeOnKeyDown` runs ⛔ IFF the builder or a descendant ⛔ HOLDS FOCUS. ⛔ MEASURED: `NativeConstruct` (`DeckBuilderWidget.cpp:545-651`) and `SelectDeckForEdit` (`:654-700`) make ⛔ ZERO focus calls · the project's ⛔ one focus idiom, `USiegeMenuInputSubsystem::ApplyInitialFocus` (`SiegeMenuInputSubsystem.cpp:365-377`), ⛔ REFUSES THE BUILDER ⛔ BY DESIGN — it opens `if (!IsMenuUncovered()) return;` and `IsMenuUncovered()` (`:171-197`) returns ⛔ false the moment any other top-level widget exists, ⛔ NAMING THE DECK BUILDER IN ITS OWN COMMENT at `:191-193` · ⛔ zero `CreateWidget|AddToViewport|SetInputMode|WBP_DeckBuilder` hits in `Source/` outside `DeckBuilderWidget.*` · the three `.uasset`s contain ⛔ ZERO occurrences of the substring `ocus` (controls fired: `Button`=7, `CreateWidget`=1, `AddToViewport`=1, `WBP_DeckBuilder`=2 — ⭐ `SC-§39`, the instrument was ⛔ proven able to return non-zero) · ⛔ live CDO read on PID 22564: `WBP_DeckBuilder_C` **`is_focusable = False`**, and `FSlateApplication::SetUserFocus` walks ⛔ leaf→root focusing the ⛔ FIRST widget that supports focus (`:3020-3036`) ⇒ ⛔ even an explicit attempt would land on an ⛔ ANCESTOR.

The bubble starts at the ⛔ FOCUSED LEAF and ⛔ decrements (`SlateApplication.cpp:388-406`) ⇒ ⛔ the leaf consumes it ⛔ before any ancestor sees it.     ⛔⛔⭐ **THE CIRCULARITY (⛔ verified independently by the orchestrator):** `MoveCardFocus` and `SetFocusedCardIndex` have ⛔ EXACTLY ONE non-test caller each — `DeckBuilderWidget.cpp:1536` and `:1547` — ⛔ BOTH INSIDE `NativeOnKeyDown`'s `Down` BRANCH. ⇒ ⛔ the grid can ⛔ only be armed from ⛔ inside the handler ⛔ no arrow key can reach. ⇒ `FocusedCardIndex` stays `INDEX_NONE` ⛔ FOREVER ⇒ `IsCardGridFocusLive()` ⛔ permanently false ⇒ Accept (`:1459`), Remove (`:1487`) and the Back-exit (`:1516`) are ⛔ ALL DEAD BEHIND IT. ✅ **This ⛔ matches the verify leg's runtime read ⛔ EXACTLY: `FocusedCardIndex == -1` through every press, ⛔ 0/34 tiles focused** (`qa/TASK-1286-verify.md`) ⇒ ⛔ the `UNOBSERVABLE` verdict was ⛔ not an instrument limit after all — it was ⛔ the defect ⛔ being observed and ⛔ not recognised.
